# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-10-02 through 2026-10-08  
**Updated:** 2026-10-02T10:46:49+00:00  
**Book-ahead watch:** through 2027-01-30

## Best date plans

### 2026-10-05 — Good Time Country Buffet + Broadway Nights Off

**Dinner, then Broadway Nights Off.** Southern buffet at Good Time Country Buffet. It is 2.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Broadway Nights Off starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=166+First+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&destination=20+Battery+Pl%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10280&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://bpca.ny.gov/event/broadway-nights-off-betsy-wolfe-2/) · [Restaurant](https://www.goodtimebuffet.com/) · [Reservations](https://blog.resy.com/2026/09/good-time-country-buffet-nyc/)

### 2026-10-04 — Atlantic Antic + Café Mars

**Atlantic Antic, then a meal.** Italian American at Café Mars. It is a 20-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Atlantic+Ave+from+Hicks+St+to+4th+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=272+3rd+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.atlanticave.org/atlanticantic) · [Restaurant](https://cafemarsbk.com/)

### 2026-10-02 — Lower East Stories + Good Time Country Buffet

**Lower East Stories, then dinner.** Southern buffet at Good Time Country Buffet. It is a 15-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 6:30 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=180+Orchard+Street&destination=166+First+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://ptknitwear.com/events/51612) · [Event booking](https://consumer.pxf.io/APvP5J?u=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.eventbrite.com%2Fe%2Flower-east-stories-tickets-2001396725482%3F%26aff%3Ddonyc) · [Restaurant](https://www.goodtimebuffet.com/) · [Reservations](https://blog.resy.com/2026/09/good-time-country-buffet-nyc/)

### 2026-10-08 — Adda Indian Canteen + Momenta Festival 2026: Keys to Creativity

**Dinner, then Momenta Festival 2026: Keys to Creativity.** Indian at Adda Indian Canteen. It is 2.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Momenta Festival 2026: Keys to Creativity starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=31-31+Thomson+Ave%2C+Long+Island+City%2C+NY+11101&destination=680+Park+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10065&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.momentaquartet.com/home/momenta-festival-2026) · [Event booking](https://www.as-coa.org/attend) · [Restaurant](https://www.addanyc.com/)

### 2026-10-03 — Café Mars + Firas Zreik's Tarabesque: New Journeys in Arabic Music

**Dinner, then Firas Zreik's Tarabesque: New Journeys in Arabic Music.** Italian American at Café Mars. It is a 12-minute walk from the venue. Firas Zreik's Tarabesque: New Journeys in Arabic Music starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=272+3rd+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=509+Atlantic+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://roulette.org/event/firas-zreik-arabic-music-rba/) · [Event booking](https://ci.ovationtix.com/36368/performance/11846560?performanceId=11846560) · [Restaurant](https://cafemarsbk.com/)

### 2026-10-03 — Outdoor Cinema at Socrates: Jean-Michel Basquiat + Adda Indian Canteen

**Outdoor Cinema at Socrates: Jean-Michel Basquiat, then dinner.** Indian at Adda Indian Canteen. It is 1.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=32-01+Vernon+Blvd%2C+Queens%2C+NY+11106&destination=31-31+Thomson+Ave%2C+Long+Island+City%2C+NY+11101&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://rooftopfilms.com/event/outdoor-cinema-at-socrates-jean-michel-basquiat/) · [Restaurant](https://www.addanyc.com/)

### 2026-10-04 — Sofreh + Comedians Interview Experts

**Dinner, then Comedians Interview Experts.** Persian at Sofreh. It is 3.0 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Comedians Interview Experts starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=75+St+Marks+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&destination=21+Clinton+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10002&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://caveat.nyc/events/comedians-interview-experts) · [Restaurant](https://www.sofrehnyc.com/)

### 2026-10-07 — Grammy-Winning ARKAI with Lindsey Stirling + Theodora

**Grammy-Winning ARKAI with Lindsey Stirling, then dinner.** Mediterranean seafood at Theodora. It is 2.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6:30 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=80+N+6th+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11249&destination=7+Greene+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://lpr.com/lpr_events/arkai26bk/) · [Event booking](https://ci.ovationtix.com/36490/production/1286065) · [Restaurant](https://www.theodoranyc.com/)

#### [Queens Night Market](https://queensnightmarket.com/)

4:00 PM · markets · Free entry; most dishes capped at a few dollars · Queens Night Market · Corona · Queens · Score 54.6  
Open-air night market behind the New York Hall of Science with about a hundred independent food, art, and merchandise vendors, deliberately kept low-priced.
**Timing:** Saturdays 4pm to midnight. Note the seasonal gap between late August and mid September.
**Practical note:** Outdoors and cash-friendly; the adjacent lot charges for parking, so the 7 train is easier.
**Why now:** Runs every Saturday evening in season; the 7 train to 111th St drops you four blocks away.
[Official](https://queensnightmarket.com/)

## Where to eat

Verified against captured public evidence for the 2026-10-02 NYC dining guide; retained useful restaurant/event cards while flagging duplicate cards, unsupported exact prices, stale “new” framing, and award wording.

Dining research: 2026-10-02T10:33:17+00:00

No candidate was verified as holding a NYC Michelin star. El Califa de León’s Michelin references apply to the original Mexico City taqueria, not the Flatiron outpost. Kirbee’s source mentions Michelin-related recognition for Texas collaborators, not a star or award for Kirbee’s.

### All-you-can-eat Southern country buffet from the Kisa/C as in Charlie team

Deals & happy hours — Good Time Country Buffet

New affordable buffet format with fried chicken, collards, mac and cheese, Salisbury steak, rotating rib tips, Sloppy Joes, chicken wings, fried catfish, chicken gizzards, and salad bar.

Price: $26.99 adults; $23.99 seniors 65+; $13.99 children ages 4-9; desserts and boozy peach frozen drinks separate  
Schedule: Wednesday-Sunday 5:00 PM-10:00 PM  
Conditions: Walk-ins plus limited Resy reservations; buffet includes repeat trips but desserts and drinks cost extra; tax/tip/service charges not stated in checked source.  
From: 2026-09-12  
Opened: 2026-09-12  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413774/good-time-country-buffet-east-village-opening-kisa)

### Black diaspora tasting menu by chef Lana Lagomarsini

Special menus — Creola

A 14-seat supper-club-style tasting menu from Bronx-born Top Chef alum Lana Lagomarsini, with dishes referencing collard-greens potlikker, Jamaican beef patties, family cookouts, and diaspora desserts.

Price: $125 tasting menu; optional $65 wine pairing  
Schedule: Open Wednesday-Saturday 5:00 PM-11:00 PM per Eater; official site says Wednesday-Saturday with two seatings nightly for a five-course seasonal menu.  
Conditions: $125 tasting menu and optional $65 wine pairing are from Eater; official homepage verified the supper-club-style, small-dining-room, five-course format but did not expose price. Sources: https://ny.eater.com/news/413465/creola-nyc-restaurant-open-lana-lagomarsini-upper-east-side-tasting-menu and https://www.creolanyc.com/  
From: 2026-09-09  
Opened: 2026-09-09  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413465/creola-nyc-restaurant-open-lana-lagomarsini-upper-east-side-tasting-menu)

### Texas barbecue collaboration from Barbs B Q and Goldee’s pitmasters

New openings — Kirbee’s

Chuck Charnichart of Barbs B Q and Jonny White of Goldee’s opened a window-order barbecue spot serving South Texas/Mexican-influenced plates and modern Texas barbecue plates, plus puddings for dessert.

Schedule: First day August 30, 11:00 AM-2:00 PM limited service; full opening Labor Day weekend, September 4-6; ongoing hours not fully extracted  
Conditions: True Texas barbecue-style line-up/window ordering; limited 24-seat dining room; items may sell out; tax/tip not stated.  
From: 2026-08-30  
Opened: 2026-08-30  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413426/kirbees-nyc-barbecue-restaurant-open-august-2026-barbs-b-q-goldees-chichuck-charnichart-jonny-white)

### Chelsea spicy Thai restaurant from the Hungry Thirsty team

More restaurants — Hungry Spicy

Chelsea restaurant from Angie Ruangphung, Phoorikun Ruangphung, and chef Prasert Kanghae, highlighting very spicy dishes from multiple Thai regions; spice levels are not adjustable.

Schedule: Opened July 2026; current hours not extracted  
Conditions: Treat as a current restaurant, not a dated pop-up or special menu. Eater says Hungry Spicy opened in July at 114 8th Avenue and spice levels cannot be adjusted. Source: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap  
Published: 2026-10-01  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap)

### Flatiron taqueria El Califa de León NYC outpost

New openings — El Califa de León

Flatiron outpost of Mexico City taqueria El Califa de León, serving a concise taco menu; Eater says the original Mexico City taqueria, not the NYC location, was Michelin-starred.

Schedule: Official site lists Monday-Sunday 11:00 AM-11:00 PM.  
Conditions: Official site exposed taco names and hours but no prices; do not imply a NYC Michelin star. Sources: https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap and https://califadeleon.com/  
Published: 2026-10-01  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/maps/best-new-nyc-restaurants-heatmap)

### Le Rock brasserie at Rockefeller Center

More restaurants — Le Rock

Official current lunch menu gives a useful Midtown option across price points, from snacks and raw-bar items to moules frites, cheeseburger deluxe, lobster roll, and classic brasserie mains.

Price: Examples checked: bread & butter $7; chickpea panisse frites $19; moules frites $32; cheeseburger deluxe $32; lobster roll $44; sole meunière $68  
Schedule: Official structured data lists Monday-Friday 11:00 AM-9:00 PM and Saturday 4:00 PM-9:00 PM; menu page description says Monday-Friday 5:30 PM-10:30 PM, so exact lunch service should be reconfirmed before going  
Conditions: Ordinary current restaurant menu, not a special deal or event. Official structured data lists Monday-Friday 11:00 AM-9:00 PM and Saturday 4:00 PM-9:00 PM; menu page text includes lunch/dinner menus. Source: https://www.lerocknyc.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.lerocknyc.com/menus/)

### The Fulton waterfront seafood restaurant at Pier 17

More restaurants — The Fulton

Official menu is useful for a Seaport seafood splurge or moderate lunch/dinner, with chowder, oysters, crudos, seafood plateau, pastas, and simply grilled fish options.

Price: Examples checked: Manhattan clam chowder cup $12/bowl $21; half-dozen oysters $26; shrimp cocktail $24; petite seafood plateau $38; Faroe Island salmon $38; black sea bass $40  
Schedule: Current hours not fully extracted from menu page; official location page linked in structured data  
Conditions: Ordinary current restaurant menu; keep separate from the one-night Chimney Rock Wine Dinner at index 23. Raw items are marked with an asterisk; tax, tip, service charges and holiday closures were not stated. Source: https://www.thefulton.nyc/menus/  
[Source](https://www.thefulton.nyc/menus/)

### Quality Bistro Midtown brasserie with $5 dressed oysters

More restaurants — Quality Bistro

Official September 2026 dinner menu includes a notably low-priced dressed oyster alongside steakhouse/brasserie dishes, useful for a bar-bites-to-splurge range near theaters and Midtown hotels.

Price: Dressed Big Rock oysters $5 each; caviar slider with Champagne bump $24; hanger steak $45; strip steak frites $58; grand shellfish plateau $165  
Schedule: Dinner menu PDF dated 6.9.26 in source; exact daily hours not extracted  
Conditions: Ordinary current restaurant menu, not a happy hour or special-menu event. The official menu lists Dressed Big Rock Oysters with Aperol Spritz granita at $5 per piece; no minimum/limit was stated. Source: https://www.qualitybistro.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.qualitybistro.com/menus/)

_No API key is needed for these public websites. `not checked` is distinct from a checked source with no final pick._

- **[arts] 92NY — not checked:** Optional source not reached.
- **[arts] Americas Society — checked, no retained pick:** Only encountered via non-arts music lead from The Skint; no arts-lane item retained.
- **[arts] Anthology Film Archives — checked, no retained pick:** Consulted calendar; fetched text exposed only page title and no usable dated listing retained.
- **[arts] Asia Society New York — not checked:** Optional source not reached.
- **[arts] Atlantic Theater Company — not checked:** Optional source not reached.
- **[arts] Balance — used:** largest source: The Skint 24/35; Brooklyn 10/35. Largest discovery source exceeds target because many official calendars were blocked or exposed sparse dynamic text; Brooklyn under 40% despite checking Brooklyn Museum, BAM, Pioneer Works, Nitehawk, Littlefield, Friends and Lovers, Greenlight, Brook…
- **[arts] BAM — checked, no retained pick:** Consulted calendar anchor and date URL; fetched text exposed general promotional copy or 404, no dated item retained.
- **[arts] BRIC — not checked:** Optional source not reached before budget was spent on mandatory and weak-day coverage.
- **[arts] Broadway.org — checked, no retained pick:** Consulted shows page; fetched text did not expose usable dated show data.
- **[arts] Brooklyn Comedy Collective — checked, no retained pick:** Consulted shows page; fetched text exposed general quote but no dated listing retained.
- **[arts] Brooklyn Magazine — checked, no retained pick:** Consulted homepage; fetched text showed subscription prompt and no dated arts event retained.
- **[arts] Brooklyn Museum — blocked (fetch failed):** Calendar and upcoming-exhibition pages returned 429; retained fall exhibition lead only via The Skint with official URL noted for manual verification.
- **[arts] Bryant Park — checked, no retained pick:** Consulted programs/activities page; fetched text exposed categories but no retained arts-lane dated event.
- **[arts] Caveat — used:** Consulted events page and event-specific pages; retained Immigrant Jam, Tom D's Big New York Show, House Rules and Comedians Interview Experts.
